```
1→"""Describe/note/reopen/focus/scan-gate handlers for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    13→    """Set augmented description on issues."""
    14→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    15→
    16→    state = host.command_runtime(args).state
    17→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    18→        return
    19→
    20→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    21→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    22→
    23→    plan = host.load_plan()
    24→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    25→    if not issue_ids:
    26→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    for fid in issue_ids:
    30→        host.describe_issue(plan, fid, text or None)
    31→    host.append_log_entry(plan, "describe", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user", detail={"text": text or None})
    32→    host.save_plan(plan)
    33→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
    34→
    35→
    36→def cmd_plan_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    37→    """Set note on issues."""
    38→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    39→
    40→    state = host.command_runtime(args).state
    41→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    42→        return
    43→
    44→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    45→    text: str | None = getattr(args, "text", None)
    46→
    47→    plan = host.load_plan()
    48→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    49→    if not issue_ids:
    50→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    for fid in issue_ids:
    54→        host.annotate_issue(plan, fid, text)
    55→    host.append_log_entry(plan, "note", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user", note=text)
    56→    host.save_plan(plan)
    57→    print(colorize(f"  Set note on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
    58→
    59→
    60→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    61→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
    62→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    63→
    64→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])

    66→    raw_state_path = host.state_path(args)

    68→        raw_state_path
    69→        if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path)
    70→        else Path(raw_state_path)
    71→        if raw_state_path
    72→        else None
    73→    )
    74→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    75→    plan_file = host._plan_file_for_state(state_file)
    76→
    77→    reopened: list[str] = []
    78→    for pattern in patterns:
    79→        reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open"))
    80→
```

```
1→"""Skip and unskip command handlers for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    12→
    13→
    14→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    15→    *,
    16→    kind: str,
    17→    attestation: str | None,
    18→    note: str | None,
    19→) -> bool:
    20→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    21→
    22→    if not host.skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
    23→        return True
    24→    if not host.validate_attestation(attestation):
    25→        host.show_attestation_requirement(
    26→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
    27→            attestation,
    28→            host.ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    29→        )
    30→        return False
    31→    if host.skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    32→        print(
    33→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    41→    *,
    42→    kind: str,
    43→    state_file: Path | None,
    44→    issue_ids: list[str],
    45→    note: str | None,
    46→    attestation: str | None,
    47→) -> dict | None:
    48→    status = _skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    49→    if status is None:
    50→        return None
    51→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    52→    for fid in issue_ids:
    53→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
    54→            state_data,
    55→            fid,
    56→            status,
    57→            note or "",
    58→            attestation=attestation,
    59→        )
    60→    return state_data
    61→
    62→
    63→def _skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
    64→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    65→
    66→    return host.skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    71→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    72→
    73→    runtime = host.command_runtime(args)
    74→    state = runtime.state
    75→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    79→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    80→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
```

```
    74→}

    76→
    77→
    78→def _try_load_prepared_packet() -> dict | None:
    79→    """Load a prepared holistic packet from query.json if it looks valid.
    80→
    81→    Returns the packet dict when query.json exists, parses as JSON, and
    82→    contains the ``investigation_batches`` key written by ``--prepare``.
    83→    Returns ``None`` otherwise (missing file, parse error, wrong shape).
    84→    """
    85→    try:
    86→        qf = query_file_path()
    87→        if not qf.exists():
    88→            return None
    89→        data = json.loads(qf.read_text())
    90→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, RuntimeError):
    91→        return None
    92→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    93→        return None
    94→    if "investigation_batches" not in data:
    95→        return None
    96→    batches = data["investigation_batches"]
    97→    if not isinstance(batches, list) or not batches:
    98→        return None
    99→    return data
   100→
```

```
    42→def _render_subjective_dimension(item: dict, *, explain: bool) -> None:
    43→    """Render a subjective dimension re-review item."""
    44→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
    45→    subjective_score = float(
    46→        detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))

    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
    53→            "cyan",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(colorize(
    57→        "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
    58→        "dim",
    59→    ))
    60→    print_user_message(
    61→        "Hey — this is a subjective review item. Run"
    62→        " `desloppify review --run-batches --dry-run`"
    63→        " to generate prompt files (one per batch)."
    64→        " Launch one subagent per prompt, all in"
    65→        " parallel. Then import with `--import-run"
    66→        " <run-dir> --scan-after-import`. Batches are"
    67→        " pre-defined — do NOT regroup them yourself."
    68→    )
    69→    if explain:
    70→        reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
    71→            "policy",
    72→            "subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.",
    73→        )
    74→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))

    77→def _render_issue_detail(
    78→    item: dict, *, single_item: bool = False, header_showed_plan: bool = False,
    79→) -> dict:
    80→    """Render plan overrides, file info, and detail fields. Returns parsed detail dict."""
    81→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    82→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
    83→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
    84→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
    85→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
    86→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
    87→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
    88→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
    89→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
    90→        steps = plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []
    91→        if steps and single_item and not header_showed_plan:
    92→            print(colorize("\n  Steps:", "dim"))
    93→            for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    94→                print(colorize(f"    {i}. {_step_text(step)}", "dim"))
    95→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    96→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
    97→
    98→    file_val = item.get("file", "")
    99→    if file_val and file_val != ".":
   100→        print(f"  File: {file_val}")
   101→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   102→
   103→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   104→    if isinstance(detail, str):
   105→        detail = {"suggestion": detail}
   106→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
   107→        detail.setdefault("lines", [])
   108→        detail.setdefault("line", None)
   109→        detail.setdefault("category", None)
   110→        detail.setdefault("importers", None)
   111→        detail.setdefault("count", 0)
   112→    if detail.get("lines"):

   114→    if detail.get("category"):
   115→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   116→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   117→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   118→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   119→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   120→
   121→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   122→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   123→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   124→        if snippet:
   125→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   126→            print(snippet)
   127→
   128→    return detail

   146→_KIND_RENDERERS = {
   147→    "cluster": _render_cluster_item,

   149→    "workflow_action": _render_workflow_action,
   150→}

   152→
   153→def _render_item_type(item: dict) -> None:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector")
   155→    if detector == "review":
   156→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   157→        return
   158→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   159→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   160→
   161→
   162→def _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item: dict, issues_scoped: dict) -> None:
   163→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   164→    if not is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   165→        return
   166→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   167→    similar_count = sum(
   168→        1
   169→        for issue in issues_scoped.values()
   170→        if issue.get("detector") == detector_name and issue["status"] == "open"
   171→    )
   172→    if similar_count <= 1:
   173→        return
   174→    print(
   175→        colorize(
   176→            f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar issues. "
   177→            f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   178→            "cyan",
   179→        )
   180→    )
   181→
   182→
   183→def _render_item_explain(
   184→    item: dict, detail: dict, confidence: str, dim_scores: dict,
   185→) -> None:
   186→    _render_item_explain_impl(
   187→        item,
   188→        detail,
   189→        confidence,
   190→        dim_scores,
   191→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   192→        get_dimension_for_detector_fn=get_dimension_for_detector,
   193→    )
   194→
   195→
   196→def _render_item(
   197→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, issues_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   198→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   199→    single_item: bool = False,
   200→    header_showed_plan: bool = False,
   201→) -> None:
   202→    kind = item.get("kind")
   203→    kind_renderer = _KIND_RENDERERS.get(kind)
   204→    if kind_renderer is not None:
   205→        kind_renderer(item)
   206→        return
   207→
   208→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   209→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   210→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   211→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   212→    _render_item_type(item)
   213→
   214→    if item.get("kind", "issue") == "subjective_dimension":
   215→        _render_subjective_dimension(item, explain=explain)
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    detail = _render_issue_detail(
   219→        item, single_item=single_item, header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   220→    )
   221→    _render_score_impact(item, dim_scores, potentials)
   222→    _render_auto_fix_batch_hint(item, issues_scoped)
   223→    if explain:
   224→        _render_item_explain(item, detail, confidence, dim_scores)
   225→
   226→
   227→def _item_label(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> str:
   228→    queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   229→    if queue_pos and total > 1:
   230→        return f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   231→    if total > 1:
   232→        return f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}]"
   233→    pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   234→    return f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   235→
   236→
   237→def render_terminal_items(
   238→    items: list[dict],
   239→    dim_scores: dict,
   240→    issues_scoped: dict,
   241→    *,
   242→    group: str,
   243→    explain: bool,
   244→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   245→    plan: dict | None = None,
   246→    cluster_filter: str | None = None,
   247→) -> None:
   248→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster or cluster filter
   249→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   250→    header_showed_plan = False
   251→    if effective_cluster and plan:
   252→        cluster_name = effective_cluster
   253→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   254→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   255→        total = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   256→        desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   257→        print(colorize(f"\n  ┌─ Cluster: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining) ─┐", "cyan"))
   258→        if desc:
   259→            print(colorize(f"  │ {desc}", "cyan"))
   260→        steps = cluster_data.get("action_steps") or []
   261→        if steps:
   262→            print(colorize("  │", "cyan"))
   263→            print(colorize("  │ Action plan:", "cyan"))
   264→            for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   265→                print(colorize(f"  │   {i}. {_step_text(step)}", "cyan"))
   266→        print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 60 + "┘", "cyan"))
   267→        print(colorize("  Back to full queue: desloppify next", "dim"))
   268→        if steps:
   269→            print(colorize(f"  Mark step done: desloppify plan cluster update {cluster_name} --done-step N", "dim"))
   270→        header_showed_plan = True
   271→
   272→    if group != "item":
   273→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   274→        return
   275→
   276→    # Detect cluster drill-in: multiple items with cluster focus active
   277→    is_cluster_drill = len(items) > 1 and (
   278→        cluster_filter or (plan and plan.get("active_cluster"))
   279→    )
   280→
   281→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   282→        if idx > 0:
   283→            print()
   284→        # Full card for first item, compact for rest in cluster drill-in
   285→        if is_cluster_drill and idx > 0:
   286→            _render_compact_item(item, idx, len(items))
   287→            continue
   288→        label = _item_label(item, idx, len(items))
   289→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   290→        _render_item(
   291→            item, dim_scores, issues_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials,
   292→            single_item=len(items) == 1,
   293→            header_showed_plan=header_showed_plan,
   294→        )
   295→
   296→
   297→__all__ = [
   298→    "render_terminal_items",
   299→]
   300→
```

```
1→"""Subjective-score helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→
     9→from .render_support import scorecard_subjective as _scorecard_subjective_impl
    10→
    11→
    12→def _scorecard_subjective(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    13→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    14→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    18→    state: dict,
    19→    dim_scores: dict,
    20→    *,
    21→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    22→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    23→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    24→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    25→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    26→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    27→            continue
    28→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    29→        if strict_val < threshold:
    30→            low.append(
    31→                (
    32→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    33→                    strict_val,
    34→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
    35→                )
    36→            )
    37→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    38→    return low
    39→
    40→
    41→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "_scorecard_subjective"]
    42→
```

```
     2→

     4→

    11→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    26→from .cluster_ops_display import _cmd_cluster_show

    28→from .cluster_ops_manage import _cmd_cluster_delete
    29→from .cluster_ops_manage import _cmd_cluster_export
    30→from .cluster_ops_manage import _cmd_cluster_import
    31→from .cluster_ops_manage import _cmd_cluster_merge
    32→from .cluster_ops_reorder import _cmd_cluster_reorder
    33→from .cluster_update import cmd_cluster_update as _cmd_cluster_update_impl
    34→
    35→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{8}$")
    36→
    37→
    38→def _suggest_close_matches(state: dict, plan: dict | None, patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    39→    """Print fuzzy match suggestions for patterns that resolved to zero issues."""
    40→    all_ids: list[str] = list(state.get("issues", {}).keys())
    41→    if plan is not None:
    42→        seen_ids: set[str] = set(all_ids)
    43→        for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    44→            if fid not in seen_ids:
    45→                seen_ids.add(fid)
    46→                all_ids.append(fid)
    47→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    48→            for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    49→                if fid not in seen_ids:
    50→                    seen_ids.add(fid)
    51→                    all_ids.append(fid)
    52→
    53→    for pattern in patterns:
    54→        segments = pattern.split("::")
    55→        last_seg = segments[-1]
    56→        suggestions: list[str] = []
    57→
    58→        if _HEX8_RE.match(last_seg):
    59→            suggestions = [fid for fid in all_ids if fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}") or fid == last_seg]
    60→            if suggestions:
    61→                print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    62→                print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    63→                for match in suggestions[:3]:
    64→                    print(colorize(f"    {match}", "dim"))
    65→                print(colorize(f"  Tip: match by hash suffix alone: {last_seg}", "dim"))
    66→            continue
    67→
    68→        slug = segments[-2] if len(segments) >= 2 else ""
    69→        for fid in all_ids:
    70→            if f"::{last_seg}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{last_seg}"):
    71→                suggestions.append(fid)
    72→            elif slug and (f"::{slug}::" in fid or fid.endswith(f"::{slug}")):
    73→                suggestions.append(fid)
    74→        if suggestions:
    75→            print(colorize(f"  No match for: {pattern!r}", "yellow"))
    76→            print(colorize("  Did you mean:", "dim"))
    77→            for match in suggestions[:3]:
    78→                print(colorize(f"    {match}", "dim"))
    79→
    80→
    81→def _print_pattern_hints() -> None:
    82→    """Print valid pattern format hints after a no-match error."""
    83→    print(colorize("  Valid patterns:", "dim"))
    84→    print(colorize("    f41b3eb7              (8-char hash suffix from dashboard)", "dim"))
    85→    print(colorize("    review::path::name    (ID prefix)", "dim"))
    86→    print(colorize("    review                (all issues from detector)", "dim"))
    87→    print(colorize("    src/foo.py            (all issues in file)", "dim"))
    88→    print(colorize("    timing_attack         (issue name — last ::segment of ID)", "dim"))
    89→    print(colorize("    review::*naming*      (glob pattern)", "dim"))
    90→    print(colorize("    my-cluster            (cluster name — expands to members)", "dim"))
    91→
    92→
    93→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    94→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    95→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    96→        return
    97→
    98→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    99→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   100→    dry_run: bool = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
   101→
   102→    plan = load_plan()
   103→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   104→    if not issue_ids:
   105→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   106→        _print_pattern_hints()
   107→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    if dry_run:
   111→        print(colorize(f"  [dry-run] Would add {len(issue_ids)} item(s) to {cluster_name}:", "cyan"))
   112→        for fid in issue_ids:
   113→            print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    try:
   117→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)
   118→    except ValueError as ex:
   119→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    member_set = set(issue_ids)
   123→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   124→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
   125→            continue
   126→        other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   127→        if not other_ids:
   128→            continue
   129→        overlap = member_set & other_ids
   130→        if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:
   131→            percent = int(len(overlap) / len(other_ids) * 100)
   132→            print(
   133→                colorize(
   134→                    f"  Warning: {len(overlap)} issue(s) also in cluster '{other_name}' "
   135→                    f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {percent}% overlap).",
   136→                    "yellow",
   137→                )
   138→            )
   139→
   140→    append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_add", issue_ids=issue_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user")
   141→    save_plan(plan)
   142→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   143→
   144→
   145→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   146→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   147→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   148→        return
   149→
   150→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   151→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   152→    dry_run: bool = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
   153→
   154→    plan = load_plan()
   155→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   156→    if not issue_ids:
   157→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   158→        _print_pattern_hints()
   159→        _suggest_close_matches(state, plan, patterns)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    if dry_run:
   163→        print(colorize(f"  [dry-run] Would remove {len(issue_ids)} item(s) from {cluster_name}:", "cyan"))
   164→        for fid in issue_ids:
   165→            print(colorize(f"    {fid}", "dim"))
   166→        return
   167→
   168→    try:
   169→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, issue_ids)
   170→    except ValueError as ex:
   171→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    append_log_entry(plan, "cluster_remove", issue_ids=issue_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user")
   175→    save_plan(plan)
   176→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   177→
   178→
   179→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   180→    """Update cluster description, steps, and/or priority."""
   181→    _cmd_cluster_update_impl(
   182→        args,
   183→        load_plan_fn=load_plan,
   184→        save_plan_fn=save_plan,
   185→        append_log_entry_fn=append_log_entry,
   186→        plan_lock_fn=plan_lock,
   187→        parse_steps_file_fn=parse_steps_file,
   188→        normalize_step_fn=normalize_step,
   189→        step_summary_fn=step_summary,
   190→        utc_now_fn=utc_now,
   191→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   196→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   197→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   198→    dispatch = {
   199→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   200→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   201→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   202→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   203→        "reorder": _cmd_cluster_reorder,
   204→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   205→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   206→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   207→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   208→        "export": _cmd_cluster_export,
   209→        "import": _cmd_cluster_import,
   210→    }
   211→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   212→    if handler is None:
   213→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   214→        return
   215→    handler(args)
   216→
   217→
   218→__all__ = ["_cmd_cluster_add", "_cmd_cluster_remove", "_cmd_cluster_update", "cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
```

```
    33→def print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    34→    """Print the stage progress indicator."""
    35→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    36→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    37→        if stage_name in stages:
    38→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    39→                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    40→            else:
    41→                print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    42→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    43→            print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    44→        else:
    45→            print(colorize(f"    ○ {label}", "dim"))
    46→
    47→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    48→        gaps = unenriched_clusters(plan)
    49→        manual = manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    50→        if not manual:
    51→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
    52→        elif gaps:
    53→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    54→            for name, missing in gaps:
    55→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    56→            print(colorize(f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}", "dim"))
    57→        else:
    58→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    62→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    63→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    64→    active_clusters = {name: c for name, c in clusters.items() if c.get("issue_ids")}
    65→    if active_clusters:
    66→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    67→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    68→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    71→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    72→            tags: list[str] = []
    73→            if auto:
    74→                tags.append("auto")
    75→            if desc:
    76→                tags.append("desc")
    77→            else:
    78→                tags.append("no desc")
    79→            if steps:
    80→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    81→            elif not auto:
    82→                tags.append("no steps")
    83→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    84→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    85→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
    86→
    87→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
    88→    for cluster in clusters.values():
    89→        all_clustered.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    90→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
    91→    if unclustered:
    92→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
    93→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
    94→            issue = open_issues[fid]
    95→            dim = (issue.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else ""
    96→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
    97→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
    98→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
    99→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   100→    elif open_issues:
   101→        organized, total, _ = triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
   102→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))
   103→
   104→
   105→def print_reflect_result(
   106→    *,
   107→    issue_count: int,
   108→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   109→    recurring: dict,
   110→    report: str,
   111→    is_reuse: bool,
   112→    cleared: list,
   113→    stages: dict,
   114→) -> None:
   115→    """Print reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   116→    print(colorize(f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, {len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).", "green"))
   117→    if is_reuse:
   118→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   119→        if cleared:
   120→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   121→    else:
   122→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   123→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   124→    if recurring_dims:
   125→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   126→            info = recurring[dim]
   127→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open", "dim"))
   128→
   129→    print()
   130→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐", "cyan"))
   131→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   132→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   133→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   134→    print_user_message(
   135→        "Reflect recorded. Before confirming — check the"
   136→        " subagent's report. Is it a strategy or just observe"
   137→        " restated? It should include a concrete cluster"
   138→        " blueprint: which clusters, which issues, what to skip"
   139→        " (with per-issue reasons). Confirm when the blueprint"
   140→        " is specific enough for organize to execute mechanically."
   141→    )
   142→
   143→
   144→def print_organize_result(
   145→    *,
   146→    manual_clusters: list[str],
   147→    plan: dict,
   148→    report: str,
   149→    is_reuse: bool,
   150→    cleared: list,
   151→    stages: dict,
   152→) -> None:
   153→    """Print organize stage output including cluster summary and next steps."""
   154→    print(colorize(f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).", "green"))
   155→    if is_reuse:
   156→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   157→        if cleared:
   158→            print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   159→    else:
   160→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   161→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize", "dim"))
   162→    for name in manual_clusters:
   163→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   164→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   165→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   166→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   167→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster.get('issue_ids', []))} issues, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   168→
   169→    print()
   170→    print(colorize("  ┌─ Prioritized organization (share with user) ────────────┐", "cyan"))
   171→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   172→        print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   173→    print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
   174→    print_user_message(
   175→        "Organize recorded. Before confirming — does the"
   176→        " organize output match the reflect blueprint? Clusters"
   177→        " by file area (same PR), not by theme? Step count <"
   178→        " issue count (consolidated)? Cluster names describe"
   179→        " locations, not problem types? This should read like"
   180→        " a set of PR plans."
   181→    )
   182→
   183→
   184→def print_reflect_dashboard(
   185→    si: object,
   186→    plan: dict,
   187→    *,
   188→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   189→) -> None:
   190→    """Show completed clusters, resolved issues, and recurring patterns."""
   191→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   192→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   193→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_issues", {})
   194→    open_issues = getattr(si, "open_issues", {})
   195→
   196→    if completed:
   197→        print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   198→        for cluster in completed[:10]:
   199→            name = cluster.get("name", "?")
   200→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   201→            thesis = cluster.get("thesis", "")
   202→            print(f"    {name}: {count} issues")
   203→            if thesis:
   204→                print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   205→            for step in cluster.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   206→                print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   207→        if len(completed) > 10:
   208→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   209→
   210→    if resolved:
   211→        print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved issues since last triage: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   212→        for fid, issue in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   213→            status = issue.get("status", "")
   214→            summary = issue.get("summary", "")
   215→            detail = issue.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(issue.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   216→            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   217→            print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   218→            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   219→        if len(resolved) > 10:
   220→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   221→
   222→    recurring = resolved_services.detect_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved)
   223→    if recurring:
   224→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   225→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   226→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   227→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   228→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   229→            print(colorize(f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}", "yellow"))
   230→    elif not completed and not resolved:
   231→        print(colorize("\n  First triage — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   232→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   233→        print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   234→        print(colorize("  - Which issues will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   235→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   236→
   237→
   238→def _print_dashboard_header(si: object, stages: dict, meta: dict, plan: dict) -> None:
   239→    _print_dashboard_header_impl(si, stages, meta, plan)
   240→
   241→
   242→def _print_action_guidance(stages: dict, meta: dict, si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   243→    _print_action_guidance_impl(stages, meta, si, plan)
   244→
   245→
   246→def _print_prior_stage_reports(stages: dict) -> None:
   247→    _print_prior_stage_reports_impl(stages)
   248→
   249→
   250→def _print_issues_by_dimension(open_issues: dict) -> None:
   251→    _print_issues_by_dimension_impl(open_issues)
   252→
   253→
   254→def cmd_triage_dashboard(
   255→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   256→    *,
   257→    services: TriageServices | None = None,
   258→) -> None:
   259→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   260→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
   261→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
   262→    state = runtime.state
   263→    plan = resolved_services.load_plan()
   264→    si = resolved_services.collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   265→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   266→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   267→
   268→    _print_dashboard_header(si, stages, meta, plan)
   269→    _print_action_guidance(stages, meta, si, plan)
   270→    _print_prior_stage_reports(stages)
   271→    _print_issues_by_dimension(si.open_issues)
   272→
   273→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   274→        print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan, services=resolved_services)
   275→
   276→    print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   277→
   278→
   279→def show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   280→    _show_plan_summary_impl(plan, state)
   281→
   282→
   283→__all__ = [
   284→    "cmd_triage_dashboard",
   285→    "print_organize_result",
   286→    "print_progress",
   287→    "print_reflect_dashboard",
   288→    "print_reflect_result",
   289→    "print_stage_progress",
   290→    "show_plan_summary",
   291→]
   292→
```

```
    51→    }
    52→
    53→
    54→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
```

```
1→"""Batch selection and payload extraction helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→
     7→
     8→def parse_batch_selection(raw: str | None, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
     9→    """Parse optional 1-based CSV list of batches."""
    10→    if not raw:
    11→        return list(range(batch_count))
    12→
    13→    selected: list[int] = []
    14→    seen: set[int] = set()
    15→    for token in raw.split(","):
    16→        text = token.strip()
    17→        if not text:
    18→            continue
    19→        idx_1 = int(text)
    20→        if idx_1 < 1 or idx_1 > batch_count:
    21→            raise ValueError(f"batch index {idx_1} out of range 1..{batch_count}")
    22→        idx_0 = idx_1 - 1
    23→        if idx_0 in seen:
    24→            continue
    25→        seen.add(idx_0)
    26→        selected.append(idx_0)
    27→    return selected
    28→
    29→
    30→def extract_json_payload(raw: str, *, log_fn) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    31→    """Best-effort extraction of first JSON object from agent output text."""
    32→    text = raw.strip()
    33→    if not text:
    34→        return None
    35→
    36→    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
    37→    last_decode_error: json.JSONDecodeError | None = None
    38→    for start, ch in enumerate(text):
    39→        if ch not in "{[":
    40→            continue
    41→        try:
    42→            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
    43→        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
    44→            last_decode_error = exc
    45→            continue
    46→        if (
    47→            isinstance(obj, dict)
    48→            and isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)
    49→            and isinstance(obj.get("issues"), list)
    50→        ):
    51→            return obj
    52→    if last_decode_error is not None:
    53→        log_fn(f"  batch output JSON parse failed: {last_decode_error.msg}")
    54→    else:
    55→        log_fn("  batch output JSON parse failed: no valid payload found")
    56→    return None
    57→
    58→
    59→__all__ = ["extract_json_payload", "parse_batch_selection"]
    60→
```

```
1→"""Merge-support scoring and issue-key helpers for batch review results."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import cast
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
     8→    LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY,
     9→    REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.issue_merge import (
    12→    merge_list_fields,
    13→    normalize_word_set,
    14→    pick_longer_text,
    15→    track_merged_from,
    16→)
    17→
    18→from .core_models import (
    19→    BatchDimensionNotePayload,
    20→    BatchIssuePayload,
    21→    BatchResultPayload,
    22→)
    23→from .scoring import DimensionMergeScorer
    24→
    25→_DIMENSION_SCORER = DimensionMergeScorer()
    26→
    27→
    28→def assessment_weight(
    29→    *,
    30→    dimension: str,
    31→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
    32→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
    33→) -> float:
    34→    """Evidence-weighted assessment score weight with a neutral floor.
    35→
    36→    Weighting is evidence-based and score-independent: the raw score does not
    37→    influence how much weight a batch contributes during merge.
    38→    """
    39→    note = dimension_notes.get(dimension, {})
    40→    note_evidence = len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else 0
    41→    issue_count = sum(
    42→        1
    43→        for issue in issues
    44→        if str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip() == dimension
    45→    )
    46→    return float(1 + note_evidence + issue_count)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _issue_pressure_by_dimension(
    50→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
    51→    *,
    52→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
    53→) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, int]]:
    54→    """Summarize how strongly issues should pull dimension scores down."""
    55→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.issue_pressure_by_dimension(
    56→        issues,
    57→        dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
```

## Hollow Hub Files Analysis

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here are all the **hollow hub files** (files that exist only to re-export or delegate to submodules with minimal logic):

### **Confirmed Hollow Hubs to Delete:**

#### 1. **`desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`** (142 lines)
**Status: HOLLOW HUB - DELETE**

- **Real logic: ~8 lines** (just constants `_BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD`, `_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD`)
- **Pure delegation: 134 lines** - every public function is a single-line wrapper calling `_*_impl` variants
- **Imports from submodules:**
  - `override_io.py`: re-exports `save_plan_state_transactional` 
  - `override_misc.py`: re-exports `cmd_plan_describe`, `cmd_plan_note`, `cmd_plan_focus`, `cmd_plan_reopen`, `cmd_plan_scan_gate`
  - `override_skip.py`: re-exports `cmd_plan_skip`, `cmd_plan_unskip`
  - `override_resolve_cmd.py`: re-exports `cmd_plan_resolve`
  - `override_resolve_helpers.py`: re-exports `blocked_triage_stages`, `check_cluster_guard`
- **Who imports it:**
  - `cmd.py` (main plan command entry point) - uses all exported functions
  - Test files only, which could import directly from implementation modules
  - Implementation modules themselves import it as "host" for monkeypatching in tests
- **Callers could import directly from:** `override_io.py`, `override_misc.py`, `override_skip.py`, `override_resolve_cmd.py`, `override_resolve_helpers.py` instead

---

```
   103→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""

   227→def do_run_batches(args, state, lang, state_file, config: dict | None = None) -> None:
   228→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""

   235→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds
   236→    batch_heartbeat_seconds = policy.heartbeat_seconds
   237→    batch_live_log_interval_seconds = (
   238→        max(1.0, min(batch_heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   239→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   240→        else 5.0
   241→    )
   242→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = policy.stall_kill_seconds
   243→
   244→    def _prepare_run_artifacts(*, stamp, selected_indexes, batches, packet_path, run_root, repo_root):

   248→            batches=batches,

   255→        )

   258→        return collect_batch_results(
   259→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   260→            failures=failures,
   261→            output_files=output_files,
   262→            allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   263→            extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
```

```
1→"""Helper logic for plan resolve command workflow/triage gating."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→
     7→
     8→def check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
     9→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
    10→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    11→
    12→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    13→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    14→    for pattern in patterns:
    15→        if pattern in clusters:
    16→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
    17→            ids = [
    18→                fid
    19→                for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    20→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
    21→            ]
    22→            if len(ids) == 0:
    23→                print(
    24→                    colorize(
    25→                        f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
    26→                        "yellow",
    27→                    )
    28→                )
    29→                print(colorize(f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>", "dim"))
    30→                return True
    31→            if len(ids) <= host._CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
    32→                print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
    33→                return True
    34→    return False
    35→
    36→
    37→def print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
    38→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    39→    print(
    40→        colorize(
    41→            f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
    42→            "yellow",
    43→        )
    44→    )
    45→    for fid in issue_ids:
    46→        issue = issues.get(fid, {})
    47→        summary = issue.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
    48→        detector = issue.get("detector", "?")
    49→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
    59→            "dim",
    60→        )
    61→    )
    62→
    63→
    64→def is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
    65→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item."""
    66→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
    67→
    68→
    69→def resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    70→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns."""
    71→    synthetic = [pattern for pattern in patterns if is_synthetic_id(pattern)]
    72→    remaining = [pattern for pattern in patterns if not is_synthetic_id(pattern)]
    73→    return synthetic, remaining
    74→
    75→
    76→def blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    77→    """Return triage stages that are blocked by unmet dependencies."""
    78→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    79→
    80→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    81→    present = order_set & host.TRIAGE_IDS
    82→    if not present:
    83→        return {}
    84→
    85→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
    86→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    87→
    88→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    89→    for sid, name in zip(host.TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    90→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
    91→            continue
    92→        deps = host.TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
    93→        unmet = sorted(
    94→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
    95→        )
    96→        if unmet:
    97→            blocked[sid] = unmet
    98→    return blocked
    99→
   100→
```

```
1→"""I/O and transactional persistence helpers for plan override commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import get_plan_file, plan_path_for_state
    10→
    11→
    12→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    13→    return path if path is not None else state_mod.get_state_file()
    14→
    15→
    16→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    17→    return path if path is not None else get_plan_file()
    18→
    19→
    20→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    21→    if state_file is None:
    22→        return None
    23→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    27→    if not path.exists():
    28→        return None
    29→    return path.read_text()
    30→
    31→
    32→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    33→    if snapshot is None:
    34→        try:
    35→            path.unlink()
    36→        except FileNotFoundError:
    37→            return
    38→        return
    39→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    40→
    41→
    42→def save_plan_state_transactional(
    43→    *,
    44→    plan: dict,
    45→    plan_path: Path | None,
    46→    state_data: dict,
    47→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    50→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    51→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    52→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    53→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    54→
    55→    try:
    56→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
    57→        # Keep this import-time indirection so tests monkeypatching
    58→        # override_handlers.save_plan still affect transactional writes.
    59→        from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    60→
    61→        host.save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
    62→    except Exception:
    63→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
    64→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
    65→        raise
    66→
    67→
    68→__all__ = [
    69→    "_plan_file_for_state",
    70→    "save_plan_state_transactional",
    71→]
    72→
```

```
1→"""Resolve command handler for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→
    11→from .override_resolve_helpers import blocked_triage_stages
    12→from .override_resolve_helpers import check_cluster_guard
    13→from .override_resolve_helpers import resolve_synthetic_ids
    14→
    15→
    16→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    17→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
    18→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    19→
    20→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    21→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    22→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    23→
    24→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
    25→        if not note:
    26→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
    27→            return
    28→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
    29→        args.attest = attestation
    30→
    31→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
    32→    if synthetic_ids:
    33→        plan = host.load_plan()
    34→
    35→        blocked_map = blocked_triage_stages(plan)
    36→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
    37→            if sid in blocked_map:
    38→                deps = ", ".join(dep.replace("triage::", "") for dep in blocked_map[sid])
    39→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
    40→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
    41→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
    42→                    return
    43→
    44→        gated_ids = [
    45→            sid
    46→            for sid in synthetic_ids
    47→            if sid in {host.WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID, host.WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID}
    48→        ]
    49→        if gated_ids:
    50→            force = getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)
    51→            meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    52→            triage_ever_completed = bool(meta.get("last_completed_at"))
    53→            if triage_ever_completed:
    54→                missing: set[str] = set()
    55→            else:
    56→                confirmed_stages = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
    57→                required_stages = {"observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"}
    58→                missing = required_stages - confirmed_stages
    59→
    60→            if missing and not force:
    61→                if not host.has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    62→                    host.inject_triage_stages(plan)
    63→                    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    64→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    65→                    host.save_plan(plan)
    66→
    67→                stage_order = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"]
    68→                next_stage = next((stage for stage in stage_order if stage in missing), "observe")
    69→
    70→                for wid in gated_ids:
    71→                    print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {wid} — triage not complete.", "red"))
    72→                print()
    73→
    74→                if next_stage == "observe":
    75→                    print(colorize("  You must analyze the findings before resolving this.", "yellow"))
    76→                    print(colorize("  Start by examining themes, root causes, and contradictions:", "dim"))
    77→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    78→                    print()
    79→                    print(colorize("  The report must be 100+ chars describing what you found.", "dim"))
    80→                elif next_stage == "reflect":
    81→                    print(colorize("  Observe is done. Now compare against previously completed work:", "yellow"))
    82→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
    83→                    print()
    84→                    print(colorize("  The report must mention recurring dimensions if any exist.", "dim"))
    85→                elif next_stage == "organize":
    86→                    print(colorize("  Reflect is done. Now create clusters and prioritize:", "yellow"))
    87→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    88→                    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
    89→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
    90→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."', "dim"))
    91→                    print()
    92→                    print(colorize("  All manual clusters must have descriptions and action_steps.", "dim"))
    93→                elif next_stage == "enrich":
    94→                    print(colorize("  Organize is done. Now enrich steps with detail and issue refs:", "yellow"))
    95→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
    96→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))
    97→                elif next_stage == "commit":
    98→                    print(colorize("  Enrich is done. Finalize the execution plan:", "yellow"))
    99→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   100→
```

```
    14→def confirm_organize(
    15→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    16→    plan: dict,
    17→    stages: dict,
    18→    attestation: str | None,
    19→    *,
    20→    services: TriageServices | None = None,

    24→
    25→    resolved_services = services or default_triage_services()
    26→    if "organize" not in stages:

    28→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."', "dim"))
    29→        return
    30→    if stages["organize"].get("confirmed_at"):
    31→        print(colorize("  Organize stage already confirmed.", "green"))

    33→
    34→    runtime = resolved_services.command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→

    39→
    40→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")

    50→    print(colorize("\n  Plan:", "bold"))
```

```
1→"""Skip and unskip command handlers for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.base.output.user_message import print_user_message
    12→
    13→
    14→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    15→    *,
    16→    kind: str,
    17→    attestation: str | None,
    18→    note: str | None,
    19→) -> bool:
    20→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    21→
    22→    if not host.skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
    23→        return True
    24→    if not host.validate_attestation(attestation):
    25→        host.show_attestation_requirement(
    26→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
    27→            attestation,
    28→            host.ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    29→        )
    30→        return False
    31→    if host.skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    32→        print(
    33→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    41→    *,
    42→    kind: str,
    43→    state_file: Path | None,
    44→    issue_ids: list[str],
    45→    note: str | None,
    46→    attestation: str | None,
    47→) -> dict | None:
    48→    status = _skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    49→    if status is None:
    50→        return None
    51→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    52→    for fid in issue_ids:
    53→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
    54→            state_data,
    55→            fid,
    56→            status,
    57→            note or "",
    58→            attestation=attestation,
    59→        )
    60→    return state_data
    61→
    62→
    63→def _skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
    64→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    65→
    66→    return host.skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    67→
    68→
    69→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    71→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    72→
    73→    runtime = host.command_runtime(args)
    74→    state = runtime.state
    75→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    79→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    80→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
    81→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
    82→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
    83→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    84→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    85→
    86→    kind = host.skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
    87→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
    88→        return
    89→
    90→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    91→    plan_file = host._plan_file_for_state(state_file)
    92→    plan = host.load_plan(plan_file)
    93→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    94→    if not issue_ids:
    95→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    96→        return
    97→
    98→    if len(issue_ids) > host._BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Bulk skip: {len(issue_ids)} items will be removed from the active queue.",
   102→                "yellow",
   103→            ),
   104→            file=sys.stderr,
   105→        )
   106→        if not getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   107→            raise host.CommandError(
   108→                f"Skipping {len(issue_ids)} items requires --confirm. "
   109→                "Review the items first, or skip individually."
   110→            )
   111→
   112→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   113→        kind=kind,
   114→        state_file=state_file,
   115→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   116→        note=note,
   117→        attestation=attestation,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   121→    count = host.skip_items(
   122→        plan,
   123→        issue_ids,
   124→        kind=kind,
   125→        reason=reason,
   126→        note=note,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→        review_after=review_after,
   129→        scan_count=scan_count,
   130→    )
   131→
   132→    host.append_log_entry(
   133→        plan,
   134→        "skip",
   135→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   136→        actor="user",
   137→        note=note,
   138→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   139→    )
   140→    if state_data is not None:
   141→        host._save_plan_state_transactional(
   142→            plan=plan,
   143→            plan_path=plan_file,
   144→            state_data=state_data,
   145→            state_path_value=state_file,
   146→        )
   147→    else:
   148→        host.save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   149→
   150→    print(colorize(f"  {host.SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   151→    if review_after:
   152→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   153→    print_user_message(
   154→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   155→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   156→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   157→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   158→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   159→    )
   160→
   161→
   162→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   163→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   164→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
   165→
   166→    runtime = host.command_runtime(args)
   167→    state = runtime.state
   168→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
   169→        return
   170→
   171→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   172→
   173→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   174→    plan_file = host._plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   175→    plan = host.load_plan(plan_file)
   176→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   177→    if not issue_ids:
   178→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   182→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = host.unskip_items(
   183→        plan,
   184→        issue_ids,
   185→        include_protected=include_protected,
   186→    )
   187→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]
   188→    host.append_log_entry(
   189→        plan,
   190→        "unskip",
   191→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   192→        actor="user",
   193→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   194→    )
   195→
   196→    reopened: list[str] = []
   197→    if need_reopen:
   198→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   199→        for fid in need_reopen:
   200→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   201→        host._save_plan_state_transactional(
   202→            plan=plan,
   203→            plan_path=plan_file,
   204→            state_data=state_data,
   205→            state_path_value=state_file,
   206→        )
   207→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   208→    else:
   209→        host.save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   210→
   211→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   212→    if protected_kept:
   213→        print(
   214→            colorize(
   215→                f"  Kept {len(protected_kept)} protected skip(s) "
   216→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   217→                "yellow",
   218→            )
   219→        )
   220→
   221→
   222→__all__ = [
   223→    "_apply_state_skip_resolution",
   224→    "_validate_skip_requirements",
   225→    "cmd_plan_skip",
   226→    "cmd_plan_unskip",
   227→]
   228→
```

## Rewiring Strategy Summary

The split modules need to replace their `from . import override_handlers as host` imports with direct imports from:
1. **External sources** (engine.plan, helpers.*, etc.) — these bypass override_handlers entirely
2. **Local constants** (_BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD, _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD) — move these into override_handlers.py and import from there, OR define them locally in each split module
3. **Other split modules** — import directly from sibling split modules (e.g., override_skip importing from override_resolve_helpers)

The main caller (`cmd.py`) can import the final 8 command functions directly from their split modules, or keep them in override_handlers as wrapper/dispatch functions.

```
1→"""I/O and transactional persistence helpers for plan override commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import get_plan_file, plan_path_for_state
    10→
    11→
    12→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    13→    return path if path is not None else state_mod.get_state_file()
    14→
    15→
    16→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    17→    return path if path is not None else get_plan_file()
    18→
    19→
    20→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    21→    if state_file is None:
    22→        return None
    23→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    24→
    25→
    26→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    27→    if not path.exists():
    28→        return None
    29→    return path.read_text()
    30→
    31→
    32→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    33→    if snapshot is None:
    34→        try:
    35→            path.unlink()
    36→        except FileNotFoundError:
    37→            return
    38→        return
    39→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    40→
    41→
    42→def save_plan_state_transactional(
    43→    *,
    44→    plan: dict,
    45→    plan_path: Path | None,
    46→    state_data: dict,
    47→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    50→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    51→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    52→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    53→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    54→
    55→    try:
    56→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
    57→        # Keep this import-time indirection so tests monkeypatching
    58→        # override_handlers.save_plan still affect transactional writes.
    59→        from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    60→
    61→        host.save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
    62→    except Exception:
    63→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
    64→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
    65→        raise
    66→
    67→
    68→__all__ = [
    69→    "_plan_file_for_state",
    70→    "save_plan_state_transactional",
    71→]
    72→
```
