1→"""Describe/note/reopen/focus/scan-gate handlers for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    13→    """Set augmented description on issues."""
    14→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    15→
    16→    state = host.command_runtime(args).state
    17→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    18→        return
    19→
    20→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    21→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    22→
    23→    plan = host.load_plan()
    24→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    25→    if not issue_ids:
    26→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    for fid in issue_ids:
    30→        host.describe_issue(plan, fid, text or None)
    31→    host.append_log_entry(plan, "describe", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user", detail={"text": text or None})
    32→    host.save_plan(plan)
    33→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
    34→
    35→
    36→def cmd_plan_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    37→    """Set note on issues."""
    38→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    39→
    40→    state = host.command_runtime(args).state
    41→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    42→        return
    43→
    44→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    45→    text: str | None = getattr(args, "text", None)
    46→
    47→    plan = host.load_plan()
    48→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    49→    if not issue_ids:
    50→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    for fid in issue_ids:
    54→        host.annotate_issue(plan, fid, text)
    55→    host.append_log_entry(plan, "note", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user", note=text)
    56→    host.save_plan(plan)
    57→    print(colorize(f"  Set note on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
    58→
    59→
    60→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    61→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
    62→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    63→
    64→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    65→
    66→    raw_state_path = host.state_path(args)
    67→    state_file = (
    68→        raw_state_path
    69→        if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path)
    70→        else Path(raw_state_path)
    71→        if raw_state_path
    72→        else None
    73→    )
    74→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    75→    plan_file = host._plan_file_for_state(state_file)
    76→
    77→    reopened: list[str] = []
    78→    for pattern in patterns:
    79→        reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open"))
    80→
    81→    if not reopened:
    82→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
    83→        return
    84→
    85→    plan = host.load_plan(plan_file)
    86→    host.purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
    87→
    88→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    89→    count = 0
    90→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    91→    for fid in reopened:
    92→        if fid in skipped:
    93→            skipped.pop(fid)
    94→            count += 1
    95→        if fid not in order:
    96→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
    97→            order.add(fid)
    98→            count += 1
    99→
   100→    host.append_log_entry(plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user")
   101→    host._save_plan_state_transactional(
   102→        plan=plan,
   103→        plan_path=plan_file,
   104→        state_data=state_data,
   105→        state_path_value=state_file,
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   109→    if count:
   110→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   115→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
   116→
   117→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   118→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   119→
   120→    plan = host.load_plan()
   121→    if clear_flag:
   122→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   123→        host.clear_focus(plan)
   124→        host.append_log_entry(plan, "focus", actor="user", detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev})
   125→        host.save_plan(plan)
   126→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   127→        return
   128→
   129→    if not cluster_name:
   130→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   131→        if active:
   132→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   133→        else:
   134→            print("  No active focus.")
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    try:
   138→        host.set_focus(plan, cluster_name)
   139→    except ValueError as ex:
   140→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   141→        return
   142→    host.append_log_entry(plan, "focus", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user", detail={"action": "set"})
   143→    host.save_plan(plan)
   144→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   145→
   146→
   147→def cmd_plan_scan_gate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   148→    """Check or skip the scan requirement for workflow items."""
   149→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
   150→
   151→    skip = getattr(args, "skip", False)
   152→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   153→
   154→    plan = host.load_plan()
   155→    scan_count_at_start = plan.get("scan_count_at_plan_start")
   156→
   157→    if scan_count_at_start is None:
   158→        print(colorize("  No active plan cycle (plan_start_scores not seeded).", "dim"))
   159→        print(colorize("  Scan gate is not applicable — workflow items gate themselves.", "dim"))
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    resolved_state_path = host.state_path(args)
   163→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(resolved_state_path)
   164→    current_scan_count = int(state_data.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   165→    scan_ran = current_scan_count > scan_count_at_start
   166→    scan_skipped = plan.get("scan_gate_skipped", False)
   167→
   168→    if not skip:
   169→        if scan_ran:
   170→            print(colorize("  Scan gate: PASSED", "green"))
   171→            print(colorize(f"  Scans at cycle start: {scan_count_at_start}  Current: {current_scan_count}", "dim"))
   172→        elif scan_skipped:
   173→            print(colorize("  Scan gate: SKIPPED (manually)", "yellow"))
   174→        else:
   175→            print(colorize("  Scan gate: BLOCKED", "red"))
   176→            print(colorize(f"  Scans at cycle start: {scan_count_at_start}  Current: {current_scan_count}", "dim"))
   177→            print(colorize("  Run: desloppify scan", "dim"))
   178→            print(colorize('  Or:  desloppify plan scan-gate --skip --note "reason"', "dim"))
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    if scan_ran:
   182→        print(colorize("  Scan already ran this cycle — no skip needed.", "green"))
   183→        return
   184→
   185→    if not note or len(note.strip()) < 50:
   186→        print(colorize("  --skip requires --note with at least 50 chars explaining why.", "red"))
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    plan["scan_gate_skipped"] = True
   190→    host.append_log_entry(
   191→        plan,
   192→        "scan_gate_skip",
   193→        actor="user",
   194→        note=note,
   195→        detail={
   196→            "scan_count_at_start": scan_count_at_start,
   197→            "current_scan_count": current_scan_count,
   198→        },
   199→    )
   200→    host.save_plan(plan)
   201→    print(colorize("  Scan requirement marked as satisfied (logged).", "yellow"))
   202→
   203→
   204→__all__ = [
   205→    "cmd_plan_describe",
   206→    "cmd_plan_focus",
   207→    "cmd_plan_note",
   208→    "cmd_plan_reopen",
   209→    "cmd_plan_scan_gate",
   210→]
   211→

1→"""Skip and unskip command handlers for plan overrides."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    14→def _validate_skip_requirements(
    15→    *,
    16→    kind: str,
    17→    attestation: str | None,
    18→    note: str | None,
    19→) -> bool:
    20→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    21→
    22→    if not host.skip_kind_requires_attestation(kind):
    23→        return True
    24→    if not host.validate_attestation(attestation):
    25→        host.show_attestation_requirement(
    26→            "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
    27→            attestation,
    28→            host.ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    29→        )
    30→        return False
    31→    if host.skip_kind_requires_note(kind) and not note:
    32→        print(
    33→            colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        return False
    37→    return True
    38→
    39→
    40→def _apply_state_skip_resolution(
    41→    *,
    42→    kind: str,
    43→    state_file: Path | None,
    44→    issue_ids: list[str],
    45→    note: str | None,
    46→    attestation: str | None,
    47→) -> dict | None:
    48→    status = _skip_kind_state_status(kind)
    49→    if status is None:
    50→        return None
    51→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    52→    for fid in issue_ids:
    53→        state_mod.resolve_issues(
    54→            state_data,
    55→            fid,
    56→            status,
    57→            note or "",
    58→            attestation=attestation,
    59→        )
    60→    return state_data
    61→
    62→
    63→def _skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
    64→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    65→
    66→    return host.skip_kind_state_status(kind)

    69→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    70→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
    71→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    72→
    73→    runtime = host.command_runtime(args)
    74→    state = runtime.state
    75→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    79→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
    80→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
    81→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
    82→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
    83→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    84→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    85→
    86→    kind = host.skip_kind_from_flags(permanent=permanent, false_positive=false_positive)
    87→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
    88→        return
    89→
    90→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    91→    plan_file = host._plan_file_for_state(state_file)
    92→    plan = host.load_plan(plan_file)
    93→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    94→    if not issue_ids:

    96→        return
    97→
    98→    if len(issue_ids) > host._BULK_SKIP_THRESHOLD:
    99→        print(

   102→                "yellow",
   103→            ),
   104→            file=sys.stderr,
   105→        )
   106→        if not getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   107→            raise host.CommandError(

   109→                "Review the items first, or skip individually."
   110→            )
   111→
   112→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   113→        kind=kind,
   114→        state_file=state_file,
   115→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   116→        note=note,
   117→        attestation=attestation,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   121→    count = host.skip_items(
   122→        plan,
   123→        issue_ids,
   124→        kind=kind,
   125→        reason=reason,
   126→        note=note,
   127→        attestation=attestation,
   128→        review_after=review_after,
   129→        scan_count=scan_count,
   130→    )
   131→
   132→    host.append_log_entry(
   133→        plan,
   134→        "skip",
   135→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   136→        actor="user",
   137→        note=note,
   138→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   139→    )
   140→    if state_data is not None:
   141→        host._save_plan_state_transactional(
   142→            plan=plan,
   143→            plan_path=plan_file,
   144→            state_data=state_data,
   145→            state_path_value=state_file,
   146→        )
   147→    else:
   148→        host.save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   149→
   150→    print(colorize(f"  {host.SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   151→    if review_after:
   152→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   153→    print_user_message(
   154→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   155→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   156→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   157→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   158→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   159→    )
   160→

   162→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   163→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   164→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
   165→
   166→    runtime = host.command_runtime(args)
   167→    state = runtime.state
   168→    if not host.require_completed_scan(state):
   169→        return
   170→
   171→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   172→
   173→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   174→    plan_file = host._plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   175→    plan = host.load_plan(plan_file)
   176→    issue_ids = host.resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   177→    if not issue_ids:
   178→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   179→        return
   180→
   181→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   182→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = host.unskip_items(
   183→        plan,
   184→        issue_ids,
   185→        include_protected=include_protected,
   186→    )
   187→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]

   189→        plan,
   190→        "unskip",
   191→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   192→        actor="user",
   193→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   194→    )
   195→
   196→    reopened: list[str] = []
   197→    if need_reopen:
   198→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   199→        for fid in need_reopen:
   200→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   201→        host._save_plan_state_transactional(
   202→            plan=plan,
   203→            plan_path=plan_file,
   204→            state_data=state_data,
   205→            state_path_value=state_file,
   206→        )
   207→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   208→    else:
   209→        host.save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   210→
   211→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   212→    if protected_kept:
   213→        print(
   214→            colorize(
   215→                f"  Kept {len(protected_kept)} protected skip(s) "
   216→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   217→                "yellow",
   218→            )
   219→        )

    16→def cmd_plan_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    17→    """Mark issues as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
    18→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    19→
    20→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    21→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    22→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
    23→
    24→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
    25→        if not note:
    26→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
    27→            return
    28→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
    29→        args.attest = attestation
    30→
    31→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
    32→    if synthetic_ids:
    33→        plan = host.load_plan()
    34→
    35→        blocked_map = blocked_triage_stages(plan)
    36→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
    37→            if sid in blocked_map:
    38→                deps = ", ".join(dep.replace("triage::", "") for dep in blocked_map[sid])
    39→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
    40→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
    41→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
    42→                    return
    43→
    44→        gated_ids = [
    45→            sid
    46→            for sid in synthetic_ids
    47→            if sid in {host.WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID, host.WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID}
    48→        ]
    49→        if gated_ids:
    50→            force = getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)
    51→            meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    52→            triage_ever_completed = bool(meta.get("last_completed_at"))
    53→            if triage_ever_completed:
    54→                missing: set[str] = set()
    55→            else:
    56→                confirmed_stages = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
    57→                required_stages = {"observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"}
    58→                missing = required_stages - confirmed_stages
    59→
    60→            if missing and not force:

    62→                    host.inject_triage_stages(plan)
    63→                    meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    64→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
    65→                    host.save_plan(plan)
    66→
    67→                stage_order = ["observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "commit"]
    68→                next_stage = next((stage for stage in stage_order if stage in missing), "observe")
    69→
    70→                for wid in gated_ids:

    72→                print()
    73→
    74→                if next_stage == "observe":

    77→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    78→                    print()

    80→                elif next_stage == "reflect":

    82→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
    83→                    print()

    85→                elif next_stage == "organize":

    87→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
    88→                    print(colorize("    desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>", "dim"))
    89→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step1" "step2"', "dim"))
    90→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."', "dim"))
    91→                    print()

    93→                elif next_stage == "enrich":

    95→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan cluster update <name> --update-step N --detail "sub-details"', "dim"))
    96→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --stage enrich --report "..."', "dim"))

    99→                    print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."', "dim"))

   204→
   205→    plan: dict | None = None
   206→    try:
   207→        runtime = host.command_runtime(args)
   208→        state = runtime.state
   209→        plan = host.load_plan()
   210→        if check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   211→            return
   212→    except host.PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   213→        plan = None
   214→
   215→    try:
   216→        if plan is None:
   217→            plan = host.load_plan()
   218→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   219→        cluster_name = next((pattern for pattern in patterns if pattern in clusters), None)
   220→        host.append_log_entry(
   221→            plan,
   222→            "done",
   223→            issue_ids=patterns,
   224→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   225→            actor="user",
   226→            note=note,
   227→        )
   228→        host.save_plan(plan)
   229→    except host.PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   230→        log_best_effort_failure(host.logger, "append plan resolve log entry", exc)
   231→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan resolve log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   232→
   233→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   234→        status="fixed",
   235→        patterns=patterns,
   236→        note=note,
   237→        attest=attestation,
   238→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   239→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   240→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   241→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   242→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   243→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   244→    )
   245→
   246→    host.cmd_resolve(resolve_args)
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_resolve"]
   250→

1→"""Helper logic for plan resolve command workflow/triage gating."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     6→
     7→
     8→def check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
     9→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
    10→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    11→
    12→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    13→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    14→    for pattern in patterns:
    15→        if pattern in clusters:
    16→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
    17→            ids = [
    18→                fid
    19→                for fid in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    20→                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get("status") == "open"
    21→            ]
    22→            if len(ids) == 0:
    23→                print(
    24→                    colorize(
    25→                        f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
    26→                        "yellow",
    27→                    )
    28→                )
    29→                print(colorize(f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>", "dim"))
    30→                return True
    31→            if len(ids) <= host._CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
    32→                print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
    33→                return True
    34→    return False
    35→
    36→
    37→def print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
    38→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    39→    print(
    40→        colorize(
    41→            f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
    42→            "yellow",
    43→        )
    44→    )
    45→    for fid in issue_ids:
    46→        issue = issues.get(fid, {})
    47→        summary = issue.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
    48→        detector = issue.get("detector", "?")
    49→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            "\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
    59→            "dim",
    60→        )
    61→    )
    62→
    63→
    64→def is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
    65→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item."""
    66→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
    67→
    68→
    69→def resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    70→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns."""
    71→    synthetic = [pattern for pattern in patterns if is_synthetic_id(pattern)]
    72→    remaining = [pattern for pattern in patterns if not is_synthetic_id(pattern)]
    73→    return synthetic, remaining
    74→
    75→
    76→def blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    77→    """Return triage stages that are blocked by unmet dependencies."""
    78→    from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415
    79→
    80→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    81→    present = order_set & host.TRIAGE_IDS
    82→    if not present:
    83→        return {}
    84→
    85→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
    86→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    87→
    88→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    89→    for sid, name in zip(host.TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
    90→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
    91→            continue
    92→        deps = host.TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
    93→        unmet = sorted(
    94→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
    95→        )
    96→        if unmet:
    97→            blocked[sid] = unmet
    98→    return blocked
    99→
   100→
   101→__all__ = [
   102→    "blocked_triage_stages",
   103→    "check_cluster_guard",
   104→    "resolve_synthetic_ids",
   105→]
   106→

    50→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    51→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    52→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    53→    state = runtime.state
    54→
    55→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    56→        return

    63→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"

    65→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)

    75→                print(colorize(

    77→                    f"or choose a different filename.",

    80→                return
    81→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    82→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    83→            print_agent_plan(
    84→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    85→                next_command=next_command,

    87→        except OSError as e:

    98→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    99→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""

   102→
   103→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   104→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
   105→    plan = load_plan()
   106→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   107→
   108→    # Dynamic queue count — matches what `next` and `plan queue` show.
   109→    try:
   110→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(runtime.state, plan)
   111→        queue_line = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
   112→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   113→        logger.debug("Plan show fell back to raw queue counts.", exc_info=exc)
   114→        # Fallback to raw plan data if queue build fails
   115→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   116→        queue_line = f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
   117→
   118→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   119→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
   120→    kind_counts = {
   121→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
   122→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
   123→    }
   124→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
   125→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
   126→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
   127→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   128→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   129→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
   130→
   131→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   132→
   133→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   134→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   135→    print(f"  {queue_line}")
   136→    if total_skipped:
   137→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   138→    else:
   139→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   140→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   141→    if clusters:
   142→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   143→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   144→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   145→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   146→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   147→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   148→    if described or noted:
   149→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   150→    if active:
   151→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   152→    if superseded:
   153→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   154→
   155→    # Commit tracking summary
   156→    _cfg = load_config()
   157→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   158→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   159→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   160→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   161→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   162→            print(
   163→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   164→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   165→            )
   166→
   167→
   168→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   169→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   170→    plan = load_plan()
   171→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   172→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   173→    reset_plan(plan)
   174→    append_log_entry(
   175→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   176→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   177→    )
   178→    save_plan(plan)
   179→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   180→
   181→
   182→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   183→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   184→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,

   186→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,

   190→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   191→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,

   193→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   194→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   195→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   196→    "scan-gate": cmd_plan_scan_gate,
   197→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   198→}
   199→
   200→
   201→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   202→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   203→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   204→    if plan_action is None:
   205→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   206→        return
   207→
   208→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   209→    if handler is None:
   210→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   211→        return
   212→    handler(args)
   213→
   214→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]

1→"""Tests for triage-completion and scan-completion gates on workflow items.
     2→
     3→workflow::score-checkpoint and workflow::create-plan require:
     4→  1. All 4 triage stages confirmed (observe, reflect, organize, commit)
     5→  2. A scan has run since the plan cycle started (scan_count > scan_count_at_plan_start)
     6→
     7→Both gates can be bypassed:
     8→  - Triage gate: --force-resolve with 50+ char note
     9→  - Scan gate: --force-resolve, or `plan scan-gate --skip --note "..."`
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import argparse
    15→
    16→import desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers as override_mod
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
    19→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    20→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Helpers
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→def _plan_with_workflow_item(
    29→    wid: str = WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    30→    *,
    31→    triage_complete: bool = False,
    32→    scan_count_at_start: int | None = None,
    33→    scan_gate_skipped: bool = False,
    34→) -> dict:
    35→    """Build a plan with a gated workflow item in the queue."""
    36→    plan = empty_plan()
    37→    plan["queue_order"] = [wid]
    38→    if triage_complete:
    39→        # Mimic real --complete behavior: stages archived to last_triage,
    40→        # triage_stages cleared, last_completed_at set
    41→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {
    42→            "triage_stages": {},
    43→            "last_completed_at": "2025-01-01T00:00:00Z",
    44→            "last_triage": {
    45→                "stages": {
    46→                    stage: {"report": f"test {stage}", "timestamp": "2025-01-01T00:00:00Z"}
    47→                    for stage in ("observe", "reflect", "organize")
    48→                },
    49→                "strategy": "test strategy",
    50→            },
    51→        }
    52→    if scan_count_at_start is not None:
    53→        plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = scan_count_at_start
    54→    if scan_gate_skipped:
    55→        plan["scan_gate_skipped"] = True
    56→    return plan
    57→
    58→
    59→def _state_with_scan_count(count: int = 5) -> dict:
    60→    return {
    61→        "issues": {},
    62→        "scan_count": count,
    63→        "last_scan": "2025-01-01T00:00:00Z",
    64→    }
    65→
    66→
    67→def _args(**overrides) -> argparse.Namespace:
    68→    defaults = {
    69→        "patterns": [WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID],
    70→        "attest": None,
    71→        "note": None,
    72→        "confirm": False,
    73→        "force_resolve": False,
    74→        "state": None,
    75→        "lang": None,
    76→        "path": None,
    77→        "exclude": None,
    78→    }
    79→    defaults.update(overrides)
    80→    return argparse.Namespace(**defaults)
    81→
    82→
    83→def _scan_gate_args(**overrides) -> argparse.Namespace:
    84→    defaults = {
    85→        "skip": False,
    86→        "note": None,
    87→        "state": None,
    88→        "lang": None,
    89→        "path": None,
    90→        "exclude": None,
    91→    }
    92→    defaults.update(overrides)
    93→    return argparse.Namespace(**defaults)
    94→
    95→
    96→def _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan):
    97→    """Patch load_plan/save_plan, return list of saved plans."""
    98→    monkeypatch.setattr(override_mod, "load_plan", lambda *a, **kw: plan)
    99→    saved = []
   100→    monkeypatch.setattr(override_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, *a, **kw: saved.append(p))
   101→    return saved
   102→
   103→
   104→def _mock_state(monkeypatch, state):
   105→    """Patch state_path and load_state to return our spoofed state."""
   106→    import desloppify.state as state_mod_real
   107→    monkeypatch.setattr(override_mod, "state_path", lambda args: None)
   108→    monkeypatch.setattr(state_mod_real, "load_state", lambda path=None: state)
   109→    # Also patch the module-level import used in override_handlers
   110→    monkeypatch.setattr(override_mod.state_mod, "load_state", lambda path=None: state)
   111→
   112→
   113→# ===========================================================================
   114→# Triage gate tests
   115→# ===========================================================================
   116→
   117→
   118→class TestTriageGateBlocksWorkflow:
   119→    """workflow::score-checkpoint is blocked when triage is incomplete."""
   120→
   121→    def test_blocked_when_triage_incomplete(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
   122→        plan = _plan_with_workflow_item(triage_complete=False)
   123→        _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan)
   124→
   125→        override_mod.cmd_plan_resolve(_args())
   126→
   127→        out = capsys.readouterr().out
   128→        assert "triage not complete" in out
   129→        assert "observe" in out  # next stage hint
   130→
   131→    def test_shows_next_stage_reflect(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
   132→        """When observe is done, should hint at reflect."""
   133→        plan = _plan_with_workflow_item()

1→"""Tests for cluster completion guard in plan resolve."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
     6→    _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD,
     7→    _check_cluster_guard,
     8→)
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Helpers
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→def _plan_with_cluster(name: str, issue_ids: list[str]) -> dict:
    16→    plan = empty_plan()
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    plan["clusters"][name] = {
    19→        "name": name,
    20→        "issue_ids": issue_ids,
    21→        "auto": True,
    22→        "cluster_key": f"auto::{name}",
    23→        "action": "autofix",
    24→        "user_modified": False,
    25→    }
    26→    return plan
    27→
    28→
    29→def _state_with_issues(*ids: str) -> dict:
    30→    issues = {}
    31→    for fid in ids:
    32→        issues[fid] = {
    33→            "id": fid,
    34→            "status": "open",
    35→            "detector": "test",
    36→            "file": "test.py",
    37→            "tier": 1,
    38→            "confidence": "high",
    39→            "summary": f"Issue {fid}",
    40→        }
    41→    return {"issues": issues}
    42→
    43→
    44→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45→# Tests
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→
    48→def test_cluster_guard_blocks_small_cluster():
    49→    """Clusters with <= threshold items should be blocked."""
    50→    ids = [f"f{i}" for i in range(5)]
    51→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", ids)
    52→    state = _state_with_issues(*ids)
    53→
    54→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)
    55→    assert blocked is True
    56→
    57→
    58→def test_cluster_guard_blocks_empty_cluster(capsys):
    59→    """Empty clusters should be blocked — must add items first."""
    60→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", [])
    61→    state = _state_with_issues()
    62→
    63→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)
    64→    assert blocked is True
    65→
    66→    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    67→    assert "empty" in captured.out.lower()
    68→    assert "add items" in captured.out.lower()
    69→
    70→
    71→def test_cluster_guard_allows_large_cluster():
    72→    """Clusters with > threshold items should be allowed."""
    73→    ids = [f"f{i}" for i in range(_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD + 1)]
    74→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", ids)
    75→    state = _state_with_issues(*ids)
    76→
    77→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)
    78→    assert blocked is False
    79→
    80→
    81→def test_cluster_guard_allows_non_cluster_pattern():
    82→    """Non-cluster patterns should not be blocked."""
    83→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", ["f1", "f2"])
    84→    state = _state_with_issues("f1", "f2")
    85→
    86→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["f1"], plan, state)
    87→    assert blocked is False
    88→
    89→
    90→def test_cluster_guard_at_threshold_boundary():
    91→    """Exactly threshold items should be blocked."""
    92→    ids = [f"f{i}" for i in range(_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD)]
    93→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", ids)
    94→    state = _state_with_issues(*ids)
    95→
    96→    blocked = _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)
    97→    assert blocked is True
    98→
    99→
   100→def test_cluster_guard_prints_items(capsys):
   101→    """Guard should print the items in the cluster."""
   102→    plan = _plan_with_cluster("auto/test", ["f1", "f2"])
   103→    state = _state_with_issues("f1", "f2")
   104→
   105→    _check_cluster_guard(["auto/test"], plan, state)
   106→    captured = capsys.readouterr()
   107→    assert "f1" in captured.out
   108→    assert "f2" in captured.out
   109→    assert "individually" in captured.out
   110→

1→"""Tests for triage dependency chain validation in cmd_plan_resolve."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→import desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers as override_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import _blocked_triage_stages
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    11→
    12→
    13→def _plan_with_triage_stages(*confirmed_stages: str) -> dict:
    14→    """Build a plan with all triage stages in queue, some confirmed."""
    15→    plan = empty_plan()
    16→    plan["queue_order"] = list(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    17→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    18→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    19→    for name in confirmed_stages:
    20→        stages[name] = {
    21→            "stage": name,
    22→            "report": f"Report for {name}",
    23→            "timestamp": "2025-06-01T00:00:00Z",
    24→        }
    25→    return plan
    26→
    27→
    28→def _args(**overrides) -> argparse.Namespace:
    29→    defaults = {
    30→        "patterns": [],
    31→        "attest": None,
    32→        "note": None,
    33→        "confirm": False,
    34→        "force_resolve": False,
    35→        "state": None,
    36→        "lang": None,
    37→        "path": None,
    38→        "exclude": None,
    39→    }
    40→    defaults.update(overrides)
    41→    return argparse.Namespace(**defaults)
    42→
    43→
    44→# ── Unit tests for _blocked_triage_stages ───────────────
    45→
    46→
    47→class TestBlockedTriageStages:
    48→    def test_nothing_confirmed_blocks_all_except_observe(self):
    49→        plan = _plan_with_triage_stages()
    50→        blocked = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
    51→        assert "triage::observe" not in blocked
    52→        assert blocked["triage::reflect"] == ["triage::observe"]
    53→        assert blocked["triage::organize"] == ["triage::reflect"]
    54→        assert blocked["triage::enrich"] == ["triage::organize"]
    55→        assert blocked["triage::sense-check"] == ["triage::enrich"]
    56→        assert blocked["triage::commit"] == ["triage::sense-check"]
    57→
    58→    def test_observe_confirmed_unblocks_reflect(self):
    59→        plan = _plan_with_triage_stages("observe")
    60→        blocked = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
    61→        assert "triage::observe" not in blocked
    62→        assert "triage::reflect" not in blocked
    63→        assert blocked["triage::organize"] == ["triage::reflect"]
    64→
    65→    def test_all_confirmed_returns_empty(self):
    66→        plan = _plan_with_triage_stages("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
    67→        assert _blocked_triage_stages(plan) == {}
    68→
    69→    def test_no_triage_in_queue_returns_empty(self):
    70→        plan = empty_plan()
    71→        plan["queue_order"] = ["some::issue"]
    72→        assert _blocked_triage_stages(plan) == {}
    73→
    74→
    75→# ── Integration tests through cmd_plan_resolve ────────────────
    76→
    77→
    78→def test_plan_resolve_rejects_blocked_triage_stage(monkeypatch, capsys):
    79→    """cmd_plan_resolve refuses to resolve triage::reflect when observe is incomplete."""

    18→def _seed_state() -> tuple[dict, str]:

    28→        summary="sample",

    33→
    34→

    37→    plan["queue_order"] = [issue_id]
    38→    return plan
    39→
    40→
    41→def test_save_plan_state_transactional_rolls_back_on_plan_write_failure(
    42→    tmp_path,
    43→    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    44→) -> None:

    46→    plan_file = tmp_path / "plan.json"

    50→    state_mod.save_state(initial_state, state_file)

    52→    before_state = state_file.read_text()
    53→    before_plan = plan_file.read_text()
    54→
    55→    changed_state = copy.deepcopy(initial_state)
    56→    state_mod.resolve_issues(
    57→        changed_state,
    58→        issue_id,
    59→        "wontfix",
    60→        note="intentional debt",
    61→        attestation=_ATTEST,
    62→    )
    63→    changed_plan = copy.deepcopy(initial_plan)
    64→    skip_items(
    65→        changed_plan,
    66→        [issue_id],
    67→        kind="permanent",
    68→        note="intentional debt",
    69→        attestation=_ATTEST,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→    def _boom(*_args, **_kwargs):
    73→        raise OSError("simulated plan write failure")
    74→
    75→    monkeypatch.setattr(override_handlers, "save_plan", _boom)
    76→    with pytest.raises(OSError):
    77→        override_handlers._save_plan_state_transactional(

    79→            plan_path=plan_file,
    80→            state_data=changed_state,
    81→            state_path_value=state_file,
    82→        )
    83→
    84→    assert state_file.read_text() == before_state
    85→    assert plan_file.read_text() == before_plan
    86→
    87→

    89→    tmp_path,

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture. Let me also check what `state_mod` is used for in override_handlers (it's `desloppify.state`), and understand the test file's monkeypatch patterns for `override_handlers`.

The key issue with `override_io.py` is that it currently imports back from `override_handlers` to call `host.save_plan` — this was to let tests monkeypatch `save_plan`. After eliminating override_handlers, override_io should import `save_plan` directly from `desloppify.engine.plan`, and the test in `test_plan_override_transactions.py` needs to monkeypatch the right module.

Similarly, the test files that do `monkeypatch.setattr(override_mod, "load_plan", ...)` etc. need to target the actual split modules instead.

Let me now implement all the changes. I'll start with the split modules.

**Step 1: Update override_io.py**

rollback both files on partial write failure.\"\"\"\n    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)\n    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)\n    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)\n    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)\n\n    try:\n        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)\n        # Keep this import-time indirection so tests monkeypatching\n        # override_handlers.save_plan still affect transactional writes.\n        from . import override_handlers as host  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n        host.save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)\n    except Exception:\n        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)\n        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)\n        raise\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"_plan_file_for_state\",\n    \"save_plan_state_transactional\",\n]",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"I/O and transactional persistence helpers for plan override commands.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom desloppify import state as state_mod\nfrom desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text\nfrom desloppify.engine.plan import get_plan_file, plan_path_for_state, save_plan\n\n\ndef _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:\n    return path if path is not None else state_mod.get_state_file()\n\n\ndef _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:\n    return path if path is not None else get_plan_file()\n\n\ndef _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:\n    if state_file is None:\n        return None\n    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)\n\n\ndef _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:\n    if not path.exists():\n        return None\n    return path.read_text()\n\n\ndef _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:\n    if snapshot is None:\n        try:\n            path.unlink()\n        except FileNotFoundError:\n            return\n        return\n    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)\n\n\ndef save_plan_state_transactional(\n    *,\n    plan: dict,\n    plan_path: Path | None,\n    state_data: dict,\n    state_path_value: Path | None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure.\"\"\"\n    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)\n    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)\n    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)\n    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)\n\n    try:\n        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)\n        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)\n    except Exception:\n        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)\n        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)\n        raise\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"_plan_file_for_state\",\n    \"save_plan_state_transactional\",\n]"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Helper logic for plan resolve command workflow/triage gating.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES\nfrom desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize\nfrom desloppify.engine.plan import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS\n\n_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10\n\n\ndef check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed.\"\"\"\n    clusters = plan.get(\"clusters\", {})\n    issues = state.get(\"issues\", {})\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        if pattern in clusters:\n            cluster = clusters[pattern]\n            ids = [\n                fid\n                for fid in cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n                if fid in issues and issues[fid].get(\"status\") == \"open\"\n            ]\n            if len(ids) == 0:\n                print(\n                    colorize(\n                        f\"\\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\\n\",\n                        \"yellow\",\n                    )\n                )\n                print(colorize(f\"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <issue-id>\", \"dim\"))\n                return True\n            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:\n                print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)\n                return True\n    return False\n\n\ndef print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:\n    issues = state.get(\"issues\", {})\n    print(\n        colorize(\n            f\"\\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(issue_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\\n\",\n            \"yellow\",\n        )\n    )\n    for fid in issue_ids:\n        issue = issues.get(fid, {})\n        summary = issue.get(\"summary\", \"(no summary)\")[:80]\n        detector = issue.get(\"detector\", \"?\")\n        print(f\"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}\")\n    print(\n        colorize(\n            \"\\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'\",\n            \"dim\",\n        )\n    )\n    print(\n        colorize(\n            \"  Or mark each resolved: desloppify plan resolve <id> --note '...' --confirm\\n\",\n            \"dim\",\n        )\n    )\n\n\ndef is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item.\"\"\"\n    return fid.startswith(\"triage::\") or fid.startswith(\"workflow::\") or fid.startswith(\"subjective::\")\n\n\ndef resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Separate synthetic IDs from real issue patterns.\"\"\"\n    synthetic = [pattern for pattern in patterns if is_synthetic_id(pattern)]\n    remaining = [pattern for pattern in patterns if not is_synthetic_id(pattern)]\n    return synthetic, remaining\n\n\ndef blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Return triage stages that are blocked by unmet dependencies.\"\"\"\n    order_set = set(plan.get(\"queue_order\", []))\n    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS\n    if not present:\n        return {}\n\n    confirmed = set(plan.get(\"epic_triage_meta\", {}).get(\"triage_stages\", {}).keys())\n    stage_names = (\"observe\", \"reflect\", \"organize\", \"enrich\", \"sense-check\", \"commit\")\n\n    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):\n        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:\n            continue\n        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())\n        unmet = sorted(\n            f\"triage::{dep}\" for dep in deps if f\"triage::{dep}\" in present and dep not in confirmed\n        )\n        if unmet:\n            blocked[sid] = unmet\n    return blocked\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"blocked_triage_stages\",\n    \"check_cluster_guard\",\n    \"resolve_synthetic_ids\",\n]\n"
}

Now update `test_workflow_gates.py`. The tests monkeypatch `override_mod.load_plan`, `override_mod.save_plan`, `override_mod.state_path`, `override_mod.state_mod`. These need to target the actual split modules now.

The tests call `override_mod.cmd_plan_resolve` and `override_mod.cmd_plan_scan_gate`. These now live in `override_resolve_cmd` and `override_misc` respectively. The monkeypatches need to target the modules where the names are actually looked up.

Let me think about what each test patches:
- `load_plan` and `save_plan` — used in both `override_resolve_cmd` and `override_misc`
- `state_path` — used in `override_resolve_cmd` and `override_misc`
- `state_mod` — used in `override_resolve_cmd` (via `state_mod.load_state`)

For `cmd_plan_resolve`, the imports are in `override_resolve_cmd`. For `cmd_plan_scan_gate`, the imports are in `override_misc`.

_state_with_scan_count(5)  # no new scan\n        _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan)\n        _mock_state(monkeypatch, state)\n\n       )\n        state = _state_with_scan_count(5)\n        _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan)\n        _mock_state(monkeypatch, state)\n\n        misc_mod.cmd_plan_scan_gate(_scan_gate_args())\n\n triage_complete=True,\n            scan_count_at_start=5,\n        )\n        state = _state_with_scan_count(5)\n        _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan)\n        _mock_state(monkeypatch, state)\n\n test_skip_with_long_note_succeeds(self, monkeypatch, capsys):\n        plan = _plan_with_workflow_item(\n            triage_complete=True,\n            scan_count_at_start=5,\n        )\n        state = _state_with_scan_count(5)\n        saved = _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan)\n        _mock_state(monkeypatch, state)\n\n        long_note = \"Skipping scan because I already validated all findings manually in the previous session\"\n        misc_mod.cmd_plan_scan_gate(_scan_gate_args(skip=True, note=long_note))\n\n        out = capsys.readouterr().out\n        assert \"satisfied\" in out\n           triage_complete=True,\n            scan_count_at_start=5,\n        )\n        state = _state_with_scan_count(6)  # scan already ran\n        _mock_plan_io(monkeypatch, plan)\n TestBothGatesInteraction:\n    \"\"\"Tests for triage + scan gates operating in sequence.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_triage_blocks_before_scan_gate_checked(self, monkeypatch, capsys):\n        \"\"\"Triage gate fires first — scan gate never checked.\"\"\"\n        plan = _plan_with_workflow_item(\n            triage_complete=False,\n            scan_count_at_start=5,\n        )\n        state = The test monkeypatches `override_handlers.save_plan` and calls `override_handlers._save_plan_state_transactional` and `override_handlers.cmd_plan_skip`. Now:
- `_save_plan_state_transactional` → `override_io.save_plan_state_transactional`
- `cmd_plan_skip` → `override_skip.cmd_plan_skip`
- `save_plan` needs to be patched on `override_io` (for transactional) and `override_skip` (for direct skip save)
- `resolve_ids_from_patterns` needs to be patched on `override_skip`
